Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in hypotheses theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 4 and the invariant I(A,B) = 254 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 4 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the hypotheses structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 254. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
